\begin{DoxyCode}{0}

\begin{DoxyCode}{0}
\DoxyCodeLine{\mbox{\hyperlink{CImg_8h_ab0fbaf7f7ec6b68d1307757bf7285960}{cimglist\_for}}(list,l)\ list[l].mirror(\textcolor{charliteral}{'x'});}

Pixel value type. 

Refer to the pixels value type of the images in the list. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item The {\ttfamily CImg\+List$<$\+T$>$\+::value\+\_\+type} type of a {\ttfamily CImg\+List$<$\+T$>$} is defined to be a {\ttfamily T}. It is then similar to CImg$<$\+T$>$\+::value\+\_\+type.
\item {\ttfamily CImg\+List$<$\+T$>$\+::value\+\_\+type} is actually not used in CImg methods. It has been mainly defined for compatibility with STL naming conventions. 
\end{DoxyItemize}
\end{DoxyNote}

Destructor. 

Destroy current list instance. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item Any allocated buffer is deallocated.
\item Destroying an empty list does nothing actually. 
\end{DoxyItemize}
\end{DoxyNote}

Default constructor. 

Construct a new empty list instance. \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item An empty list has no pixel data and its dimension width() is set to {\ttfamily 0}, as well as its image buffer pointer data().
\item An empty list may be reassigned afterwards, with the family of the assign() methods. In all cases, the type of pixels stays {\ttfamily T}. 
\end{DoxyItemize}
\end{DoxyNote}

Construct list containing empty images. 


\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em n} & Number of empty images. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Useful when you know by advance the number of images you want to manage, as it will allocate the right amount of memory for the list, without needs for reallocation (that may occur when starting from an empty list and inserting several images in it). 
\end{DoxyNote}

Display the current \doxylink{structcimg__library__suffixed_1_1CImgList}{CImg\+List} instance in a new display window. 


\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em disp} & Display window. \\
\hline
{\em display\+\_\+info} & Tells if image information are displayed on the standard output. \\
\hline
{\em axis} & Alignment axis for images viewing. \\
\hline
{\em align} & Apending alignment. \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
This function opens a new window with a specific title and displays the list images of the current \doxylink{structcimg__library__suffixed_1_1CImgList}{CImg\+List} instance into it. Images of the list are appended in a single temporarly image for visualization purposes. The function returns when a key is pressed or the display window is closed by the user. 
\end{DoxyNote}

Return the type of image pixel values as a C string. 

Return a {\ttfamily char\texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}} string containing the usual type name of the image pixel values (i.\+e. a stringified version of the template parameter {\ttfamily T}). \begin{DoxyNote}{Note}

\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item The returned string may contain spaces (as in {\ttfamily "{}unsigned char"{}}).
\item If the pixel type {\ttfamily T} does not correspond to a registered type, the string {\ttfamily "{}unknown"{}} is returned. 
\end{DoxyItemize}
\end{DoxyNote}
